The purpose of speaking it in that way is explained: "in order that all infinite sentient beings might attain this path" of the supreme vehicle; since, having generated the mind for the benefit of migrating beings countless eons ago, and having brought the completion, ripening, and purification to finality, after manifesting perfect awakening, out of love and great compassion, having taught this extremely profound and hard-to-hear Dharma to fortunate disciples, he establishes migrating beings pervasive as space without exception in the state of supreme awakening. How this summarizes the meaning of the entrusting is as follows: In the Eight Thousand Stanza Sūtra corresponding to this context, it says, "From this perfection of wisdom, the pristine wisdom of omniscience will be completely perfected," and so forth, showing it to be the source of the precious qualities from which the paths and fruits of the Great Vehicle without exception arise, and stating that wherever this perfection of wisdom is practiced, there the Buddha resides, the Dharma is taught, and the Saṅgha gathers, and so forth, therefore, out of all sūtras, this one must be earnestly guarded; because it was entrusted three times, from this very sūtra as well, the way the power of the phrase "In order that all sentient beings might attain this path" is brought out is from the Master's Commentary on Difficult Points: "The word 'might' means it is completely entrusted to the Noble Ānanda and others." It is said that all sentient beings attaining this path is the primary intent of the Buddha, and because that also depends on it remaining for a long time without degenerating, it was earnestly entrusted. This concludes the commentary on the thirty-second chapter, the Chapter on Entrusting, from the commentary on the Verse Summary of the Perfection of Wisdom (Ratnaguṇasañcayagāthā) called The Jeweled Lamp Fulfilling the Hopes of the Fortunate: Illuminating the Suchness of Profound Dependent Arising. ^3-335

## Concluding Topics ^4-0

![[bo-khenrab-jamyang-norbu-dronme#^4-1]]

The concluding meaning. Fourth, regarding the concluding meaning, there are two: showing the completion of the remaining text without exception, and stating the translation colophon of the translators. ^4-1

### Showing the Completion of the Text without Remainder ^4-1-0

![[bo-khenrab-jamyang-norbu-dronme#^4-2]]

Showing the completion of the remaining text without exception. The first: ^4-2

![[bo-khenrab-jamyang-norbu-dronme#^4-3]]

"The Noble Verse Summary of the Perfection of Wisdom is complete." Thus it is said. ^4-3

### Translator's Colophon ^4-2-0

![[bo-khenrab-jamyang-norbu-dronme#^4-4]]

The translation colophon. Second, the manner in which it was translated into Tibetan is: "Translated, revised, and finalized by the Indian preceptor Vidyākarasiṃha, that is, Tsöndrü Jungne Senge, and the great reviser-translator, the sole eye of the world, Bande Paltsek," and, "This Noble Verse Summary extracted from the Eighteen Thousand Stanza Perfection of Wisdom, having been compared with an Indian manuscript revised by Master Haribhadra and a pure Tibetan manuscript, was revised once again by the Zhalu translator, the monk Dharmapālabhadra, that is, Chökyong Zangpo." Thus two different translations appear. Thus, this sūtra is no different from the Teacher himself, and it should be known that as long as it remains in this world through hearing, contemplating, and meditating, the Teacher is teaching the Dharma. Furthermore, having understood the manner of being advised through many earnest exhortations, such as the fact that doing even a little activity for this precious sūtra in this degenerate time pleases and delights the Teacher himself, those who desire well-being for themselves, reflecting upon the fact that there are immeasurable temporary and ultimate benefits such as repaying the kindness of the Teacher and spontaneously accomplishing the two benefits for oneself and others, should follow the excellent biographies of the scholars and siddhas of the past, and thereby cause the meaning of the precious perfection of wisdom sūtra to flourish in the mindstreams of themselves and others, just as elucidated by the Regent, Protector Maitreya, and the two great chariot-makers Nāgārjuna and Asaṅga. ^4-4

![[bo-khenrab-jamyang-norbu-dronme#^4-5]]

Here it is said: Where all the rivers of excellent explanation of the profound and vast, whose depths and limits are unapparent, pool completely, filled with ten million jewels of hidden meaning; dense with garlands of waves of manifold reasonings, striking fear into the hearts of unlearned children, possessing the swirling depths of profound emptiness; a place where the host of ten million naga-kings, the bodhisattvas, supremely beautiful with their hoods of the jewels of scripture and realization, joyfully enter and play; victorious is the great wish-fulfilling ocean of the Mother of the Conquerors, the all-producer of the wish-fulfilling jewels of the omniscient Conquerors who spontaneously accomplish the two aims. The essential nectar churned from the milky ocean of the eighty-four thousand holy Dharmas; causing the lotus grove of the excellent paths of the three vehicles to bloom, producing the supreme fruits desired by the four noble ones; dispelling all the dark ignorance of the beings of the three levels, it is beautiful like the thousand rays of light of excellent explanation. There is nothing comparable to this illumination of the sun of the Dharma-system of the perfection of wisdom. This is the single path traversed by the Conquerors of the three times; the medicinal nectar of Dharma that cures the diseases of the three times; the wish-granting tree from which all goodness arises; the supreme guide leading to the jewel-island of the three bodies; the eye of the world that illuminates the excellent profound path; the moon possessing white light that dispels the torment of existence and peace; the king of wish-granting jewels that fulfills the hopes of the fortunate; it is also the supreme Teacher showing the path to awakening. The wondrous festival of partaking as much as one wishes in the sweet dew of the nectar of the Sage's excellent explanation, of which even the name is difficult to hear in countless aeons—Ema! Such fortune is extremely excellent! The great ocean of the profound and vast meaning of the Mother, difficult to fathom; who in this present time is able to drink it completely? Therefore, just a drop resting on the tip of the kuśa grass of my feeble intellect I have taken as my own share. The Protector Mañjughoṣa, the sun of speech, father and sons, though they illuminated the difficult points of the sutras and tantras without exception, their leaving aside a word-commentary on this text in equanimity, I think, was left precisely as my allotted share. Although it is not beneficial for foolish, ignorant, arrogant ones whose eyes of intellect are impaired and who are intoxicated by the poisonous water of sense pleasures, it is the music of excellent explanation that delights the learned, and it is the offering water that pleases all Conquerors and their Children. Nevertheless, although the intended meaning of the perfection of wisdom is vast like the sky, the intellect of an ordinary child like me is thin like a blade of grass; and since it is not illustrated by the light of ten million suns, having destroyed the darkness of the two obscurations of the minds of beings, may the illumination of omniscient pristine wisdom flourish! The wish-granting tree of the Conqueror's teachings, the basis of benefit and happiness, may its branches and leaves of explanation and practice fully expand, and by the pouring rain of inexhaustible goodness and all desires, may the hopes of beings pervading space be fulfilled! The full mandala of unmistaken view, meditation, and conduct, endowed with the thousand rays of excellent explanation that dispel the darkness of bad views, may the treasury of light of the wondrous Gelug tradition, shine continuously in the sky of the Sage's teachings forever! Gathering the supreme sweet fragrance of pure ethics, and blooming with the hundred petals of the intellect of vast learning, by the host of the white lotus garlands of countless scholars and siddhas, may the joy-grove of the Dharma-centers of explanation and practice be beautified! May I too in all my lives be cared for by the Mañjughoṣa Guru and become his chief child! Reaching the very peak of the supreme sevenfold wisdom, unforgetting dhāraṇī, and confidence, following Nāgārjuna and Asaṅga in upholding the holy Dharma of the Conquerors without exception, may I train in magnificent conduct just like Mañjuśrīghoṣa and Samantabhadra! May all embodied beings who are connected to me through seeing, hearing, remembering, or touching, never part from the spiritual friend, the guiding captain and protector, and entering the great ship of hearing, contemplating, and meditating, may they travel to the jewel-island of the three bodies! On the captivating smiling face of the goddess of the sky, as long as it is beautified by the fluttering side-glances of the sun and moon, by this lamp of jewels of excellent explanation as well, may the hopes of the fortunate disciples be fulfilled! From the expanse filled with the great clouds of the blessings of the ocean of the Conquerors of the Three Jewels, with the brahma-voice of true speech resounding like summer thunder, the rain of flowers of goodness pours down. On the earth of Samantabhadra, the wish-granting tree of the two collections fully blossoms, and the golden ears of definitive goodness are born. May it be pervaded by the glorious auspiciousness of the spontaneous accomplishment of the fruits of the two benefits for oneself and others! Thus, this so-called "Jewel Lamp Fulfilling the Hopes of the Fortunate: A Commentary on the Verse Summary of the Precious Qualities, Illuminating the Suchness of Profound Dependent Arising," was earnestly exhorted by the former throne-holder of the great Dharma center of explanation and practice, Sershul Monastery Tekchen Dargye Ling, the beautiful ornament of the teachings in Domé, the holder of the treasury of the jewels of wondrous scripture, realization, and qualities, the supreme emanation Gompa Lobsang Gelek Tendar; and also exhorted by the head chant master of the great assembly, Lobsang Rabgye, whose intellect and discernment engage broadly in the topics of sutra and tantra; and in accordance with the repeated exhortations of many holy beings, such as my own attendant, the vast-learned logician former abbot Jamyang Rinchen, and the illuminator of the Ganden teachings, the supreme emanation Thötsa Lobsang Tenzin Trinley; having enjoyed the nectar of the speech of many spiritual friends of the supreme vehicle, such as the venerable guru Lobsang Heka Pal Kalzang Chöpel Palzangpo, who has realized the intended meaning of the entirety of sutra and tantra and perfected the profound samadhis of the two stages, and the great pandita of the five sciences, the great renunciate bodhisattva venerable Lobsang Phuntsok Palzangpo; it was composed by the monk who holds the Yellow Hat tradition, Khyenrab Jamyang Lobsang Trinley, at the place where the summer thunder of Dharma of explanation and practice sounds, Ganden Shedrup Tupten Pelgye Ling, and its scribe was the former abbot Nechung Jamyang Rinchen. Om svasti. Of the hundreds of thousands of naga-kings of scholars and siddhas of Jambudvipa, resting his toenails on their jewel crown ornaments, the Dharma Lord, the second Conqueror who gives teachings, may that Omniscient One protect these beings! The roots of the difficult conduct of the bodhisattvas are firm, the branches of perfecting, maturing, and purifying are complete, the wish-granting tree endowed with the essence of the fruits of the three bodies, its producer is the perfection of wisdom, the Mother of the Conquerors. While filling all fields with light in that manner, the Protector of a hundred myriad fields, together with the miracles of samadhi, magical displays, and illumination of Dharma, proclaimed it completely to the retinue of the three types of beings. The rivers of its profound meaning without exception, pooling completely in the great ocean of the meaning of the Verse Summary of the Perfection of Wisdom, in accordance with the tradition of the great chariot of the Land of Snows, that the venerable guru has elucidated it is wondrous. In order to open a hundred doors of benefit and happiness through this, the supreme emanation Lobsang Tenzin Trinley, having provided the funds, caused this word-commentary on the Verse Summary of the Precious Qualities to be published. By the very white milky ocean of the accumulation of virtue of striving here, of stainless excellent actions accumulated in the three times, as it accompanies its friend of ten million garlands of waves, may it merge with the wish-fulfilling ocean of the three bodies! The precious teachings of the Sage in general, and in particular, those of the Dharma King of the three realms, Tsongkhapa, may his teachings of sutra and tantra remain for a long time, and may the activities of the beings who uphold them flourish! May I and the embodied beings connected to me, being born in Shambhala, the supreme field of the vidyadharas, complete the journey of the five stages of the profound path, and actualize the state of the primordial Buddha! Thus, this colophon and prayer for the printing was also requested by the sovereign of an ocean of excellent qualities, the supreme emanation Lobsang Trinley, during the publication of this commentary on the Verse Summary; and as requested, it was hand-written by the ignorant, sick one, Jamyang Rinchen. Sarvadā kalyāṇaṃ bhavantu! Om svasti. The fortunate six-legged bees in the lotus grove of the Conqueror's teachings, in order to enjoy the nectar of the honey of explanation and practice, from the Dharma enclosure of Ganden Shedrup Pel Ling, let fall this rain of the inexhaustible nectar of the continuum of Dharma! By the virtue of publishing this commentary on the Verse Summary of the Perfection of Wisdom composed by the supreme emanation of Khanang, Rinpoche Khyenrab Jamyang Lobsang Trinley Palzangpo, in the year 1985, may it become the cause for the quintessence of the Conqueror's teachings, the Yellow Hat tradition, to spread in all directions and remain for a long time! ^4-5
